# META
~~~ini
description=Model export retains effective identification, authored multiplicity expressions, exposure shape and evaluation outcomes (S4-S6, S11)
type=file
libraries=standard
require_complete_publication=true
require_no_diagnostics=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package ExportFacts {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    part def Thing;
    part def Base {
        attribute count : Natural default = 3;
        part things : Thing[count];
        part ranged : Thing[count..count + 2];
    }
    part instance : Base { attribute :>> count = 4; }
    attribute sum : Integer = 2 + 3;
    calc def Double { in x : Real; return : Real = x * 2; }
    attribute doubled : Real = Double(1.0);
    attribute sequenceSum : ScalarValue = NumericalFunctions::sum((1, 2));
    attribute definitionClass = Base meta SysML::PartDefinition;
    attribute input : Real;
    attribute nonconstant : Real = input + 1;
    constraint condition { 1 < 2 }
    view shallow { expose Base::*; }
    view recursive { expose Base::**; }
}
~~~
